## Frequently Asked Questions

Find answers to common questions about Black & White Stays Wayanad

##### What types of accommodations do you offer in Wayanad?

We offer three distinct accommodation experiences: 'Nestle with Nature' for serene mountain views, 'Hustle in Hispeed' with luxurious amenities for business travelers, and 'Bustle in Budget' for affordable group workations. All our properties are centrally located in Kalpetta with easy access to Wayanad's top attractions.

##### What tourist attractions are nearby?

Our location provides easy access to Wayanad's diverse attractions including tea plantations, waterfalls like Thusharagiri and Meenmutty, wildlife sanctuaries, romantic spots like Pookode Lake, cultural sites like Edakkal Caves, adventure activities like trekking and cycling, local markets for spice shopping, and pilgrimage sites.

##### Do you provide workation facilities?

Yes! We specialize in workations with our 'Colive, Cowork & Coplay' concept. Our properties offer high-speed internet, dedicated workspaces, and a peaceful environment perfect for remote work. The scenic Western Ghats setting provides the ideal backdrop for increased productivity and motivation.

##### What is the best time to visit Wayanad?

Wayanad is beautiful year-round, but the best time is October to May when the weather is pleasant for sightseeing. The monsoon season (June-September) offers lush greenery and spectacular waterfalls, perfect for nature lovers. Our misty mountain location provides a cool climate throughout the year.

##### How do I make a reservation?

You can make a reservation by clicking the 'Reservation' button on our website, contacting us through the contact form, or reaching out via WhatsApp. We'll help you choose the perfect accommodation based on your needs - whether it's a family vacation, romantic getaway, or business workation.

##### What amenities are included in your stays?

Our accommodations include modern amenities such as high-speed WiFi, comfortable furnishing, kitchen facilities, scenic views of tea estates and mountains, proximity to local attractions, and 24/7 support. Specific amenities vary by property type - contact us for detailed information about your chosen accommodation.

##### Are you suitable for families with children?

Absolutely! We welcome families and provide a safe, comfortable environment for children. Wayanad offers numerous family-friendly attractions including museums, wildlife sanctuaries, farm visits, and cultural sites. Our central location makes it easy to access child-friendly activities and restaurants.

##### What outdoor activities can I enjoy in Wayanad?

Wayanad offers diverse outdoor activities including trekking to Chembra Peak, cycling through tea estates, bamboo rafting, wildlife safaris, waterfall visits, plantation tours, zip-lining, and exploring tribal villages. Our location provides easy access to all these adventure activities and sporting venues.

### Pilgrimage

? 4.34 km from Kalpetta

- Transportation: Public

- Type: Pilgrimage
- Fee : NA
- Duration :
- Holiday: NA

Anantnath Swami Temple (also known as the Puliyarmala Jain Temple is a Jain temple located at Puliyarmala, 6 km from Kalpetta in the Wayanad district in the state of Kerala in India .Anantnath Swami Temple (also known as the Puliyarmala Jain Temple) dedicated to Lord Anantanatha, the 14th Tirthankara.

## About us

#### Stay in the heart of hill station

- Walk Everywhere

Steps from Food Joins, City Center, and Tourist locations.

- Stay Inspired

Best nightlife, shopping, and cultural attractions at your doorstep.

- Unmatched Connectivity

Quick access to Cab Stand,Bus Station, and Transport

- Best for:

First-time visitors, business travelers, and those who want to be in the center of it all.

## About us

#### Stay in the heart of hill station

- Walk Everywhere

Steps from Food Joins, City Center, and Tourist locations.

- Stay Inspired

Best nightlife, shopping, and cultural attractions at your doorstep.

- Unmatched Connectivity

Quick access to Cab Stand,Bus Station, and Transport

- Best for:

First-time visitors, business travelers, and those who want to be in the center of it all.

Thirunelli Temple (also spelled Tirunelli) is an ancient temple dedicated to Maha Vishnu on the side of Brahmagiri hill in the Wayanad district in the state of Kerala in India

## 900 Kandi Glass Bridge
#### Discover your Leisure

![900 Kandi Glass Bridge   ](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/romantic/bw-stays-900-thollayiram-kandi-wayanad.webp)![900 Kandi Glass Bridge   ](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/romantic/bw-weekend-destination-glassbridge-wayanad.webp)

### 900 Kandi Glass Bridge

[View places within 15 Kms](https://www.bwstays.com/bw-900-kandi-romantic-wayanad.html#nearby-places)

? 3.921km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 9:00 am – 18:00


- Transportation: Private

- Best Seasons to Visit: Summer
(February-May), Monsoon (June-September), Winter
(October-January)

- Type: Romantic
- Fee : 100-250 INR & 4\*4 Jeep drive to top ?1200
- Holiday: NA
- Contact: +91 62385 76424/+91 97444 82007

- ![Black and White Stays Wayanad](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

The Wayanad Glass bridge is spotted at a distance of 900m from
Thollayiram Kandi and is approximately 13km from Meppadi.

The 900 Kandi Glass Bridge delivers a captivating experience, blending heart-pounding adventure with breathtaking views of nature. Suspended high above lush valleys and green landscapes, this glass bridge offers a one-of-a-kind journey that will leave you in awe.

### Attamala View Point

[View places within 15 Kms](https://www.bwstays.com/bw-attamala-view-point-romantic-wayanad.html#nearby-places)

? 4.625km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 9:00 am – 18:30

- Transportation: Public
- Best Seasons to Visit: Summer (February-May), Monsoon (June-September), Winter (October-January)
- Type: Romantic
- Fee :
- Duration : 2 hours
- Holiday: Sun
- Contact: +91 493 000 0000
- ![Black and White Stays Wayanad](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

Attamala Viewpoint is a scenic viewpoint located near Chooralmala in the Wayanad district of Kerala. It is one of the hidden places to visit during your Wayanad tour.
At a distance of 15 km from Kalpetta, and 39 km from Sulthan Bathery, Attamala View Point is a stunning viewpoint located in the Kottathara panchayath of Vythiri.

## Bamboo Forest Park
#### Discover your Leisure

![Bamboo Forest Valentine's Park    ](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/romantic/bw-workation-bamboo-park-wayanad.webp)

### Bamboo Forest Valentine's Park

[View places within 15 Kms](https://www.bwstays.com/bw-bamboo-forest-valentine-park-wayanad.html#nearby-places)

? 3.221 km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 8:00 am – 18:00

- Transportation: Public
- Best Seasons to Visit: Summer (February-May), Monsoon (June-September), Winter (October-January)
- Type: Romantic
- Fee : 50 INR
- Duration : 1 Hour
- Holiday: Sun
- Contact: +91 75919 33369
- ![holiday luxury villa](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

Bamboo Forest Park, Wayanad is a hidden gem nestled in the heart of Kerala's lush landscape. Known for its serene ambiance and unspoiled natural beauty, the park offers a tranquil escape from the hustle of mainstream tourist spots. Surrounded by thick bamboo groves and teeming with local flora and fauna, it's an ideal place for nature walks, birdwatching, and moments of quiet reflection. Whether you're a nature lover, a photographer, or just someone seeking peace, Bamboo Forest Park invites you to slow down and soak in the calm of Wayanad’s wilderness.

## About us

#### Stay in the heart of hill station

- Walk Everywhere

Steps from Food Joins, City Center, and Tourist locations.

- Stay Inspired

Best nightlife, shopping, and cultural attractions at your doorstep.

- Unmatched Connectivity

Quick access to Cab Stand,Bus Station, and Transport

- Best for:

First-time visitors, business travelers, and those who want to be in the center of it all.

### Lakkidi Ghats View Point

[View places within 15 Kms](https://www.bwstays.com/bw-lakkidi-ghats-view-point-wayanad.html#nearby-places)

? 4.516 km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 6:00 am – 17:00

- Transportation: Public
- Best Seasons to Visit: Summer (February-May), Monsoon (June-September), Winter (October-January)
- Type: Trucking

- Duration :
- Holiday: Sun

- ![Black and White Stays Wayanad](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

Lakkidi View Point is a tourist destination often referred to as the “Gateway of Wayanad.” It's known for its mesmerising bird's eye view of Wayanad.

### Pakshipathalam wild life sanctuary

[View places within 15 Kms](https://www.bwstays.com/bw-pakshipathalam-wildlife-sanctuary-wayanad.html#nearby-places)

? 4.342 km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 7:00 am – 17:00


- Transportation: Public

- Best Seasons to Visit: Summer
(February-May), Monsoon (June-September), Winter
(October-January)

- Type: Wildlife
- Fee :
- Duration :
- Holiday: NA
- Contact: +91 493 000 0000

- ![Black and White Stays Wayanad](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

For bird lovers, this Pakshipathalam trek offers a myriad of
bird species on its 7 km stretch in the deep forests of
Wayanad. The Forest Resort at Lanternstay provides guided
tours through various treks; thus, it is family friendly and
the adventures here are always safe and enjoyable. On this
stretch, there are possibilities to sighting some rare
species, such as Malabar Grey Hornbill and migratory birds.

### Ambalavayal Farm

[View places within 15 Kms](https://www.bwstays.com/bw-ambalavayal-farm-rars-wayanad.html#nearby-places)

? 4.511 km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 9:00 am – 16:30

- Transportation: Public
- Best Seasons to Visit: Summer (February-May), Monsoon (June-September), Winter (October-January)
- Type: Farm
- Fee :
- Duration :
- Holiday: NA
- Contact: +91 4936 260 421
- ![Black and White Stays Wayanad](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

Regional Agricultural Research Station for High range zone is located at Ambalavayal Wayanad district. Ambalavayal is located about 25 km east of Kalpetta, the district headquarter. The station was established under Ex Service men Colonisation programme Wayanad, in 1946 by the then Madras Government.

## Arripara Waterfalls Wayanad
#### Discover your Leisure

![Arripara falls ](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/falls/bw-stays-arripa-falls-kalpetta.webp)

### Arripara falls

[View places within 15 Kms](https://www.bwstays.com/bw-arripara-waterfall-wayanad.html#nearby-places)

? 4.337 km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 9:00 am – 17:00

- Transportation: Public
- Best Seasons to Visit: Summer (February-May), Monsoon (June-September), Winter (October-January)
- Type: Falls
- Fee : 10 INR
- Duration : 1-2 Hours
- Holiday: NA
- Contact: NA
- ![Black and White Stays Wayanad](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

Arippara Waterfalls on Iruvanjipuzha River is located near Nellipoyil in Thiruvambadi panchayat of Kozhikode district.. The waterfall is on one of the tributaries of Iruvanji River commonly known as Iruvanjipuzha.This place can be visited along with Thusharagiri Waterfall (11 Kms) & Pathankayam Waterfalls (3 Kms)

### Banasurasagar Dam

[View places within 15 Kms](https://www.bwstays.com/bw-banasurasagar-dam-romantic-wayanad.html#nearby-places)

? 4.321 km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 9:00 am – 17:30

- Transportation: Public
- Best Seasons to Visit: Summer (February-May), Monsoon (June-September), Winter (October-January)
- Type: Romantic
- Fee :
- Duration :
- Holiday: NA
- Contact: +91 493 627 3460
- ![Black and White Stays Wayanad](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

It is the largest earthen dam in India and the second largest in Asia and a starting point for hikes into the surrounding mountains. It is an important tourist attraction near Mananthavady, Wayanad.Banasura Sagar Dam is a picturesque tourist hotspot in Wayanad. Its sprawling reservoir of clear water is surrounded by lush green hills and it’s dotted with tiny islands.

### Chain Tree

[View places within 15 Kms](https://www.bwstays.com/bw-chain-tree-cultural-heritage-wayanad.html#nearby-places)

? 4.215 km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 9:00 am – 17:00

### Cheeyambam waterfalls

[View places within 15 Kms](https://www.bwstays.com/bw-cheeyambam-waterfall-wayanad.html#nearby-places)

? 4.332 km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 9:00 am – 17:00

- Transportation: Public
- Best Seasons to Visit: Summer (February-May), Monsoon (June-September), Winter (October-January)
- Type: Falls
- Fee :
- Duration :
- Holiday: NA
- Contact: NA
- ![Black and White Stays Wayanad](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

Chethalayam Waterfall is quietly emerging as a gem among the natural wonders of Wayanad, capturing the attention of discerning travellers who seek tranquility and stunning vistas. Tucked away in Northern Kerala, this enchanting waterfall remains one of the region's hidden treasures, celebrated for its breathtaking beauty and pristine environment.

### Coffee Plantation

[View places within 15 Kms](https://www.bwstays.com/bw-coffee-plantation-plantation-wayanad.html#nearby-places)

? 4.07km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 09:00-17:00

- Transportation: Public
- Best Seasons to Visit: Summer (February-May), Monsoon (June-September), Winter (October-January)
- Type: Plantations
- Fee :
- Duration :
- Holiday: Sun
- Contact: +91-000 000 0000
- ![Black and White Stays Wayanad](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

Wayanad, Kerala, is known for its coffee plantations, particularly Robusta, which accounts for a significant portion of the state's coffee production. These plantations, often nestled among shade trees, contribute significantly to the local economy and are a popular destination for tourists seeking to experience the coffee-making process

- Contact: +91 493 621 5339
- ![Black and White Stays Wayanad](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

Edakkal Caves is an enchanting site with archaeological importance. It’s known for its mystic rock formation and ancient drawings and engravings. And these petroglyphs are some of the earliest examples of human writing and expression.

Edakkal means “a stone in between.” And this cave-like rock shelter is formed by a massive boulder wedged between two bigger boulders. It’s believed to exist for over 8,000 years now..

Edakkal Caves' story is shrouded in mystery. As per the legends, the caves were formed because of Luv and Kush – sons of Lord Rama. It’s said that their arrows split a large rock into two, thus forming this narrow passage.

The earliest Edakkal Caves carvings are believed to be from the neolithic era (the Stone Age), as early as 6000 BCE. The latest carvings are from around 1000 BCE.

These carvings were discovered only recently, in the 19th century. Fred Fawcett, a police official of the erstwhile Malabar state, found these during one of his hunting trips.

## En Ooru Tribal Village
#### Discover your Leisure

![tribal ](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/tribal/bw-homestay-en-ooru-tribal-heritage-village-wayanad.webp)

### Tribal

[View places within 15 Kms](https://www.bwstays.com/bw-en-ooru-tribal-wayanad.html#nearby-places)

? 4.116 km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 9:00 am – 17:00

- Transportation: Public
- Best Seasons to Visit: Summer (February-May), Monsoon (June-September), Winter (October-January)
- Type: Tribal
- Fee :
- Duration :
- Holiday: NA
- Contact: +91 493 000 0000
- ![En ooru Tribal Village](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

A village reflects the indigenous culture and traditional lifestyle of the Ethnic Community of the place. En Ooru, the first tribal heritage village in Kerala, introduce indigenous architecture coupled with tribal customs and traditions. Opened at Pookode in Wayanad district by the State Government, En Ooru is spread across 25 acres of land on the slope of a hillock.

### Kanthanpara Falls

[View places within 15 Kms](https://www.bwstays.com/bw-kanthanpara-waterfall-wayanad.html#nearby-places)

? 4.118 km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 9:00 am – 17:00

- Transportation: Public
- Best Seasons to Visit: Summer (February-May), Monsoon (June-September), Winter (October-January)
- Type: Falls
- Fee :
- Duration :
- Holiday: NA
- Contact: NA
- ![Black and White Stays Wayanad](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

Kanthanpara is a picturesque waterfall situated near Meppadi, coursing down from a relatively shorter height of about 100 ft

### Karapuzha Dam

[View places within 15 Kms](https://www.bwstays.com/bw-karapuzha-dam-romantic-wayanad.html#nearby-places)

? 4.316 km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 9:00 am – 18:00

- Transportation: Public
- Best Seasons to Visit: Summer (February-May), Monsoon (June-September), Winter (October-January)
- Type: Romantic
- Fee :
- Duration :
- Holiday: Sun
- Contact: +91 493 000 0000
- ![Black and White Stays Wayanad](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

Karapuzha Dam is a prominent earthen dam in Wayanad, Kerala, India, known for its scenic beauty and recreational facilities. It is one of the largest earthen dams in India. The dam, located on the Karapuzha River, a tributary of the Kabini River, is a popular destination for tourists and nature enthusiasts.

## About us

#### Stay in the heart of hill station

- Walk Everywhere

Steps from Food Joins, City Center, and Tourist locations.

- Stay Inspired

Best nightlife, shopping, and cultural attractions at your doorstep.

- Unmatched Connectivity

Quick access to Cab Stand,Bus Station, and Transport

- Best for:

First-time visitors, business travelers, and those who want to be in the center of it all.

## About us

#### Stay in the heart of hill station

- Walk Everywhere

Steps from Food Joins, City Center, and Tourist locations.

- Stay Inspired

Best nightlife, shopping, and cultural attractions at your doorstep.

- Unmatched Connectivity

Quick access to Cab Stand,Bus Station, and Transport

- Best for:

First-time visitors, business travelers, and those who want to be in the center of it all.

### Mailadippara Romantic

- Duration :

- ![traditional experiences vs modern amenities in wayanad stays](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

## About us

#### Stay in the heart of hill station

- Walk Everywhere

Steps from Food Joins, City Center, and Tourist locations.

- Stay Inspired

Best nightlife, shopping, and cultural attractions at your doorstep.

- Unmatched Connectivity

Quick access to Cab Stand,Bus Station, and Transport

- Best for:

First-time visitors, business travelers, and those who want to be in the center of it all.

### Meenmutty Falls

[View places within 15 Kms](https://www.bwstays.com/bw-meenmutty-waterfall-wayanad.html#nearby-places)

? 4.241 km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 9:00 am – 4:00

- Type: Falls
- Fee :
- Duration :
- Holiday: NA

- ![pool features contribute to tranquil environments ](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

Meenmutty Waterfalls is a beautiful and serene place hidden amidst forests, and it’s a prime attraction for adventure seekers. From the top, you can see the entire valley below and the surrounding mountains. The trek through the calming natural beauty is an experience in itself

## Phantom Rock Getaway
#### Discover your Leisure

![Phantom rock  ](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/romantic/bw-resort-phanthom-rock-wayanad.webp)

### Phantom rock

[View places within 15 Kms](https://www.bwstays.com/bw-phanthom-rock-romantic-wayanad.html#nearby-places)

? 3.919km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 9:00 am – 17:00

- Transportation: Public
- Best Seasons to Visit: Summer (February-May), Monsoon (June-September), Winter (October-January)
- Type: Romantic
- Fee :
- Duration :
- Holiday: Sun
- Contact: +91 493 000 0000
- ![adventurous activities or relaxing getaways finding the best stay options](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

Phantom Rock is situated 2600 feet above sea level, on the way to Ambalavayal from Wayanad. The rock resembles the skull of a human and its name is inspired by the evergreen Phantom comics. Locally the place is called 'Cheengeri Mala.' There are well-connected roads to reach this place, but the last 500 meters is a trek on foot.

## About us

#### Stay in the heart of hill station

- Walk Everywhere

Steps from Food Joins, City Center, and Tourist locations.

- Stay Inspired

Best nightlife, shopping, and cultural attractions at your doorstep.

- Unmatched Connectivity

Quick access to Cab Stand,Bus Station, and Transport

- Best for:

First-time visitors, business travelers, and those who want to be in the center of it all.

? 4.113 km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 9:00 am – 17:00

- Transportation: Public

- Type: Romantic
- Fee :
- Duration :
- Holiday: Sun

- ![Black and White Stays Wayanad](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

### Sentinel Rock Waterfall

[View places within 15 Kms](https://www.bwstays.com/bw-sentinel-rock-waterfall-wayanad.html#nearby-places)

? 4.023 km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 9:00 am – 4:00

- Transportation: Public
- Best Seasons to Visit: Summer (February-May), Monsoon (June-September), Winter (October-January)
- Type: Falls
- Fee :
- Duration :
- Holiday: NA
- Contact: NA
- ![Black and White Stays Wayanad](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

Soochipara Falls also known as Sentinel Rock Waterfalls is a three-tiered waterfall in Vellarimala, Wayanad, India. It is surrounded by deciduous, evergreen and montane forests. Locally referred to as Soochipara ("Soochi" meaning "Needle" and "Para" meaning "Rock"), the 15-20 minute drive from Meppadi to Sentinel Rock Waterfalls has views of a number of Wayanad's tea estates

## Thusharagiri Falls
#### Discover your Leisure

![Thusharagiri falls   ](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/falls/bw-stays-thusharagiri-falls-service-apartment.webp)

### Thusharagiri falls

[View places within 15 Kms](https://www.bwstays.com/bw-thusharagiri-waterfall-wayanad.html#nearby-places)

? 4.334 km from Kalpetta

- Timing: 9:00 am – 17:00

- Transportation: Public
- Best Seasons to Visit: Summer (February-May), Monsoon (June-September), Winter (October-January)
- Type: Falls
- Fee :
- Duration :
- Holiday: NA
- Contact: NA
- ![Black and White Stays Wayanad](https://www.bwstays.com/assets/img/icons/cloudy.webp)Weather:

Thusharagiri Falls is a captivating waterfall located in Kozhikode, Kerala, known for its three-tiered cascade and the misty spray that gives it its name, "snow-capped peak". It's a popular destination for nature lovers and trekkers due to its scenic beauty, trekking trails, and rich biodiversity.

## Frequently Asked Questions

Find answers to common questions about Black & White Stays Wayanad

##### What types of accommodations do you offer in Wayanad?

We offer three distinct accommodation experiences: 'Nestle with Nature' for serene mountain views, 'Hustle in Hispeed' with luxurious amenities for business travelers, and 'Bustle in Budget' for affordable group workations. All our properties are centrally located in Kalpetta with easy access to Wayanad's top attractions.

##### What tourist attractions are nearby?

Our location provides easy access to Wayanad's diverse attractions including tea plantations, waterfalls like Thusharagiri and Meenmutty, wildlife sanctuaries, romantic spots like Pookode Lake, cultural sites like Edakkal Caves, adventure activities like trekking and cycling, local markets for spice shopping, and pilgrimage sites.

##### Do you provide workation facilities?

Yes! We specialize in workations with our 'Colive, Cowork & Coplay' concept. Our properties offer high-speed internet, dedicated workspaces, and a peaceful environment perfect for remote work. The scenic Western Ghats setting provides the ideal backdrop for increased productivity and motivation.

##### What is the best time to visit Wayanad?

Wayanad is beautiful year-round, but the best time is October to May when the weather is pleasant for sightseeing. The monsoon season (June-September) offers lush greenery and spectacular waterfalls, perfect for nature lovers. Our misty mountain location provides a cool climate throughout the year.

##### How do I make a reservation?

You can make a reservation by clicking the 'Reservation' button on our website, contacting us through the contact form, or reaching out via WhatsApp. We'll help you choose the perfect accommodation based on your needs - whether it's a family vacation, romantic getaway, or business workation.

##### What amenities are included in your stays?

Our accommodations include modern amenities such as high-speed WiFi, comfortable furnishing, kitchen facilities, scenic views of tea estates and mountains, proximity to local attractions, and 24/7 support. Specific amenities vary by property type - contact us for detailed information about your chosen accommodation.

##### Are you suitable for families with children?

Absolutely! We welcome families and provide a safe, comfortable environment for children. Wayanad offers numerous family-friendly attractions including museums, wildlife sanctuaries, farm visits, and cultural sites. Our central location makes it easy to access child-friendly activities and restaurants.

##### What outdoor activities can I enjoy in Wayanad?

Wayanad offers diverse outdoor activities including trekking to Chembra Peak, cycling through tea estates, bamboo rafting, wildlife safaris, waterfall visits, plantation tours, zip-lining, and exploring tribal villages. Our location provides easy access to all these adventure activities and sporting venues.
